ADCC silver medalist, IBJJF and Abu Dhabi World Pro black belt world champion &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/people/21078-felipe-pena\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Felipe &s;Preguica&s; Pena&l;/a&g; will go up against EBI champion &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/people/21718-gordon-ryan\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Gordon Ryan&l;/a&g; in a 60-minute, submission-only superfight. &l;br&g;&l;br&g;Pena is one of the most talented black belts active in jiu-jitsu with victories in many organizations around the world. Ryan is the brash up-and-comer who has made a name from himself by winning many tournaments and superfights, including a submission win over Keenan Cornelius. &l;br&g;&l;br&g;In total, three members of the &s;Danaher Death Squad&s; will face off against highly-respected opponents from various teams. &l;br&g;&l;br&g;&l;/span&g;&l;strong&g;&l;u&g;Also featured on the card:&l;/u&g;&l;/strong&g;&l;br&g;&l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/people/21065-garry-tonon\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Garry Tonon&l;/a&g; faces off against &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/people/21389-kim-terra\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Kim Terra&l;/a&g; in a no time-limit submission-only match. Tonon has become infamous for his constant searching out of only the toughest superfight challenges. With leglock submission wins over elite black belts (including the likes of Ralek Gracie, Gilbert Burns, Dustin Akbari, Masakazu Imanari and Marcin Held) Tonon will bring his all-out-attacking style to face off with the crafty technician Kim Terra. The younger brother of world champion Caio Terra, Terra has an unorthodox and deceptive no-gi game that should pose an interesting clash of styles with Tonon&s;s game. &l;br&g;&l;br&g;Rounding off the event is a special teen no-gi submission-only match between &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/people/21802-kennedy-maciel\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Kennedy Maciel&l;/a&g; and &l;a href=\&q;http://www.flograppling.com/people/23278-nicky-ryan\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Nicky Ryan&l;/a&g;. This match features Maciel -- son of legendary world champion &s;Cobrinha&s; -- go up against Gordon Ryan&s;s 15-year-old brother, Nicky. Maciel is a creative and technical grappler much like his father. 540 will be the location for a highly anticipated encounter! ADCC silver medalist, IBJJF and Abu Dhabi World Pro black belt world champion Felipe &s;Preguica&s; Pena will go up against EBI champion Gordon Ryan in a 60-minute, submission-only superfight.&q;,&q;description&q;:&q;&l;span style=\&q;background-color: initial;\&q;&g;Studio 540 will be the location for a highly anticipated encounter! ADCC silver medalist, IBJJF and Abu Dhabi World Pro black belt world champion &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/people/21078-felipe-pena\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Felipe &s;Preguica&s; Pena&l;/a&g; will go up against EBI champion &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/people/21718-gordon-ryan\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Gordon Ryan&l;/a&g; in a 60-minute, submission-only superfight. &l;br&g;&l;br&g;Pena is one of the most talented black belts active in jiu-jitsu with victories in many organizations around the world. Ryan is the brash up-and-comer who has made a name from himself by winning many tournaments and superfights, including a submission win over Keenan Cornelius. &l;br&g;&l;br&g;In total, three members of the &s;Danaher Death Squad&s; will face off against highly-respected opponents from various teams. &l;br&g;&l;br&g;&l;/span&g;&l;strong&g;&l;u&g;Also featured on the card:&l;/u&g;&l;/strong&g;&l;br&g;&l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/people/21065-garry-tonon\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Garry Tonon&l;/a&g; faces off against &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/people/21389-kim-terra\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Kim Terra&l;/a&g; in a no time-limit submission-only match. Tonon has become infamous for his constant searching out of only the toughest superfight challenges. With leglock submission wins over elite black belts (including the likes of Ralek Gracie, Gilbert Burns, Dustin Akbari, Masakazu Imanari and Marcin Held) Tonon will bring his all-out-attacking style to face off with the crafty technician Kim Terra. The younger brother of world champion Caio Terra, Terra has an unorthodox and deceptive no-gi game that should pose an interesting clash of styles with Tonon&s;s game. &l;br&g;&l;br&g;Rounding off the event is a special teen no-gi submission-only match between &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/people/21802-kennedy-maciel\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Kennedy Maciel&l;/a&g; and &l;a href=\&q;http://www.flograppling.com/people/23278-nicky-ryan\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Nicky Ryan&l;/a&g;. This match features Maciel -- son of legendary world champion &s;Cobrinha&s; -- go up against Gordon Ryan&s;s 15-year-old brother, Nicky. Maciel is a creative and technical grappler much like his father. 540 will be the location for a highly anticipated encounter! ADCC silver medalist, IBJJF and Abu Dhabi World Pro black belt world champion Felipe &s;Preguica&s; Pena will go up against EBI champion Gordon Ryan in a 60-minute, submission-only superfight.&q;,&q;description&q;:&q;&l;span style=\&q;background-color: initial;\&q;&g;Studio 540 will be the location for a highly anticipated encounter! ADCC silver medalist, IBJJF and Abu Dhabi World Pro black belt world champion &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/people/21078-felipe-pena\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Felipe &s;Preguica&s; Pena&l;/a&g; will go up against EBI champion &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/people/21718-gordon-ryan\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Gordon Ryan&l;/a&g; in a 60-minute, submission-only superfight. &l;br&g;&l;br&g;Pena is one of the most talented black belts active in jiu-jitsu with victories in many organizations around the world. Ryan is the brash up-and-comer who has made a name from himself by winning many tournaments and superfights, including a submission win over Keenan Cornelius. &l;br&g;&l;br&g;In total, three members of the &s;Danaher Death Squad&s; will face off against highly-respected opponents from various teams. &l;br&g;&l;br&g;&l;/span&g;&l;strong&g;&l;u&g;Also featured on the card:&l;/u&g;&l;/strong&g;&l;br&g;&l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/people/21065-garry-tonon\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Garry Tonon&l;/a&g; faces off against &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/people/21389-kim-terra\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Kim Terra&l;/a&g; in a no time-limit submission-only match. Tonon has become infamous for his constant searching out of only the toughest superfight challenges. With leglock submission wins over elite black belts (including the likes of Ralek Gracie, Gilbert Burns, Dustin Akbari, Masakazu Imanari and Marcin Held) Tonon will bring his all-out-attacking style to face off with the crafty technician Kim Terra. The younger brother of world champion Caio Terra, Terra has an unorthodox and deceptive no-gi game that should pose an interesting clash of styles with Tonon&s;s game. &l;br&g;&l;br&g;Rounding off the event is a special teen no-gi submission-only match between &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/people/21802-kennedy-maciel\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Kennedy Maciel&l;/a&g; and &l;a href=\&q;http://www.flograppling.com/people/23278-nicky-ryan\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Nicky Ryan&l;/a&g;. This match features Maciel -- son of legendary world champion &s;Cobrinha&s; -- go up against Gordon Ryan&s;s 15-year-old brother, Nicky. Maciel is a creative and technical grappler much like his father. Lo is at a turning point. How he handles the challenge in front of him will likely dictate the future of his career. &l;/p&g;&q;,&q;content&q;:&q;&l;p&g;Leandro Lo is at a turning point. How he handles the challenge in front of him will likely dictate the future of his career.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;It&a;#39;s never a good moment when a legend starts to falter, and Leandro Lo is very much one of the legendary champions of our sport. Since 2012 his name has been featured at the forefront of the game. We&a;#39;ve become accustomed to seeing him hit the podium whenever he competes, but as late his results have not been as expected. &a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;A black belt world champion in three different weight classes (technically four &a;mdash; we&a;#39;ll get into that in a minute) Lo has long been referenced as one of the best competitors of his era, and the stats certainly back that up. Lo is a six-time IBJJF World champion and has earned titles in four different weight classes. Technically, you could argue that he won five titles in three divisions &a;ndash; Buchecha gifted him the absolute gold in 2018 when Lo injured his shoulder and couldn&a;#39;t fight in the final.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;But a look at the stats shows that time might be catching up with Leandro, who turned 30 this year. He&a;#39;s been increasingly less active, and his win ratio is at an all-time low.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;table border=\&q;1\&q; cellpadding=\&q;0\&q; cellspacing=\&q;0\&q; data-sheets-value=&s;{\&q;1\&q;:3,\&q;3\&q;:1}&s;&g;1&l;/td&g;&l;td data-sheets-value=&s;{\&q;1\&q;:3,\&q;3\&q;:5}&s;&g;5&l;/td&g;&l;/tr&g;&l;tr&g;&l;td data-sheets-value=&s;{\&q;1\&q;:2,\&q;2\&q;:\&q;Win ratio: \&q;}&s;&g;Win ratio:&a;nbsp;&l;/td&g;&l;td data-sheets-numberformat=&s;[null,3,\&q;0.0%\&q;,1]&s; data-sheets-value=&s;{\&q;1\&q;:3,\&q;3\&q;:0.827}&s;&g;82.7%&l;/td&g;&l;td data-sheets-numberformat=&s;[null,3,\&q;0.0%\&q;,1]&s; data-sheets-value=&s;{\&q;1\&q;:3,\&q;3\&q;:0.764}&s;&g;76.4%&l;/td&g;&l;td data-sheets-numberformat=&s;[null,3,\&q;0.0%\&q;,1]&s; data-sheets-value=&s;{\&q;1\&q;:3,\&q;3\&q;:0.88}&s;&g;88.0%&l;/td&g;&l;td data-sheets-numberformat=&s;[null,3,\&q;0.0%\&q;,1]&s; data-sheets-value=&s;{\&q;1\&q;:3,\&q;3\&q;:0.883}&s;&g;88.3%&l;/td&g;&l;td data-sheets-numberformat=&s;[null,3,\&q;0.0%\&q;,1]&s; data-sheets-value=&s;{\&q;1\&q;:3,\&q;3\&q;:0.896}&s;&g;89.6%&l;/td&g;&l;td data-sheets-numberformat=&s;[null,3,\&q;0.0%\&q;,1]&s; data-sheets-value=&s;{\&q;1\&q;:3,\&q;3\&q;:0.945}&s;&g;94.5%&l;/td&g;&l;td data-sheets-numberformat=&s;[null,3,\&q;0.0%\&q;,1]&s; data-sheets-value=&s;{\&q;1\&q;:3,\&q;3\&q;:0.895}&s;&g;89.5%&l;/td&g;&l;td data-sheets-numberformat=&s;[null,3,\&q;0.0%\&q;,1]&s; data-sheets-value=&s;{\&q;1\&q;:3,\&q;3\&q;:0.928}&s;&g;92.8%&l;/td&g;&l;td data-sheets-numberformat=&s;[null,3,\&q;0.0%\&q;,1]&s; data-sheets-value=&s;{\&q;1\&q;:3,\&q;3\&q;:0.687}&s;&g;68.7%&l;/td&g;&l;/tr&g;&l;/tbody&g;&l;/table&g;&l;p&g;&l;br&g;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;Looking at his record (stats courtesy of &l;a href=\&q;https://www.bjjheroes.com/bjj-fighters/leandro-lo-pereira\&q; rel=\&q;noopener noreferrer\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;BJJHeroes.com&l;/a&g;) we can see that Lo has had 292 black belt matches in his career. That&a;#39;s nearly double that of fellow legend Marcus &a;quot;Buchecha&a;quot; Almeida, who has 152, and still far more than the likes of prolific black belts Keenan Cornelius or the Miyao brothers, none of whom has more than 250 matches. In the last two years, he has competed less than ever &a;ndash; only 30 matches across 2018 and 2019. He averaged 37 matches a year from 2011 to 2017, with a peak of 48 matches in 2017.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;The slowdown is due to a number of reasons. Lo dislocated his shoulder in the final versus Mahamed Aly in June of 2018, an injury that took him out for the rest of the year. He&a;#39;s become more selective of the events in which he chose to compete, preferring major tournaments such as Worlds and Pans and taking fewer superfights.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;Lo is still top-tier wherever and whenever he steps on the mat. It takes a special kind of talent to beat him, but the list of opponents who have managed just that is growing at an alarming rate.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;In 16 matches this year Lo has won just 11. He started the year off losing to adversary Nicholas Meregali in his first match back since his shoulder injury. A shock submission loss to Adam Wardzinski followed at Pans, as did a referee decision to Lucas &a;quot;Hulk&a;quot; Barbosa. Kaynan Duarte beat him in the final at Worlds, passing what was once considered a practically-impassable guard, and most recently former student Gustavo &a;quot;Braguinha&a;quot; Batista handed him a loss at BJJ Stars.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;That&a;#39;s not to say it&a;#39;s been all bad for Lo. This year he still beat Keenan Cornelius, submitted Luiz Panza, and dealt losses to Vinicius &a;quot;Trator&a;quot; Ferreira, Rudson Mateus, Dominique Bell, and Jackson Sousa.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;Lo isn&a;#39;t done, but the new generation of standout competitors has closed the gap, and Lo needs to evolve to stay ahead of the pack. Much of his game was built on a series of finely honed techniques, impeccable timing, a limitless gas tank and an ingrained ability to pull out the win when necessary. As Lo moved up in weight he retained some of his signature moves (including his deadly knee-cut pass) but began to rely more on wrestling than his notable sweeping sequences. Instead of evolving, it appears that his game has devolved to some degree &a;ndash; a handful of techniques that work, but that are predictable and therefore countered more easily.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;Leandro competed at BJJ Stars 2, narrowly winning the opening match against Dimitrius Souza of Alliance before losing to Batista. This was in Lo&a;#39;s hometown of Sao Paulo, where he counts massive local support and has historically always performed well.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;It seems Leandro is at a turning point in his career. He&a;#39;s so well-known that nothing he does is a surprise. People know his winning techniques inside-out, and his fabled explosiveness and athleticism aren&a;#39;t quite as potent as they used to be. An erratic work ethic in the gym coupled with a fast lifestyle has dulled his edge. The younger generation can match and even beat his physical talents, and they have the technical answers to his game.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;It&a;#39;s time to evolve. All of the GOATs have had to do it. Look at Andre Galvao or Xande Ribeiro. Their styles are drastically different from when they were younger, but they were able to adapt and even improve their jiu-jitsu so that they remained at the world-class level past the age of 35.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;It&a;#39;s a harsh reality for a professional athlete to admit they&a;#39;re getting older, but Leandro has to make some changes if he wants to stay relevant as we move into the 2020 gi season.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;hr&g;&l;h2&g;Results Recap:&l;/h2&g;&l;p&g;Kaynan Duarte was superb in the main event of Fight 2 Win 128 against Nick Rodriguez. &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/articles/6584259-kaynan-duarte-first-person-to-tame-wild-animal-nick-rodriguez\&q; rel=\&q;noopener noreferrer\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Find out more here&l;/a&g;.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;BJJ Stars proved to be an eventful night, with Nicholas Meregali crowned champion of their Heavyweight Grand Prix. &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/articles/6584686-nicholas-meregali-mauls-competition-to-win-bjj-stars\&q; rel=\&q;noopener noreferrer\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;More about BJJ Stars here&l;/a&g;.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;The most shocking news out of Rome and the IBJJF European No-Gi Championship was that Paulo Miyao went out in the quarterfinal of the featherweight division to Gabriel Sousa of ZR Team, who would go on to take gold.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;All in all it was a good trip for Unity Jiu-Jitsu and the associated Cicero Costha team:&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;ul class=\&q;froala-ul\&q;&g;&l;li&g;Jonas Andrade took gold at light-featherweight by beating Rodnei Barbosa&a;nbsp;&l;/li&g;&l;li&g;Jeferson Guaresi saw off Diego Ramalho for gold at middleweight&a;nbsp;&l;/li&g;&l;li&g;Devhonte Johnson won gold at super-heavyweight by defeating Helton Junior of Atos&l;/li&g;&l;/ul&g;&l;p&g;Adam Wardzinski took double gold in the go at the IBJJF Rome Fall Open, winning both the heavyweight and absolute divisions. He should also get two medals: one for his flowing locks, and another for his sweet mustache. Rock on, Adam.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;&l;span class=\&q;fr-video fr-fvc fr-dvi fr-draggable\&q; contenteditable=\&q;false\&q;&g;&l;iframe class=\&q;fr-instagram embed fr-draggable\&q; scrolling=\&q;no\&q; src=\&q;https://www.instagram.com/p/B3xHnqkH_Od/embed/captioned/?v=7\&q; style=\&q;border: 0; margin: 0; max-width: 658px; width: 100%; display: block; padding: 0; background: rgb(255, 255, 255);\&q; height=\&q;1023\&q;&g;&l;/iframe&g;&l;/span&g;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;&l;br&g;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;Matthew Leighton took gold in the absolute division of the IBJJF Twin Cities Open with a win over ultra-heavyweight Thomas McMahon of Alliance. The TAC Team black belt will appear on &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/events/6520742-fight-2-win-129\&q; rel=\&q;noopener noreferrer\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Fight 2 Win 129&l;/a&g; in Chicago. He faces Mauricio Oliveira of GF Team.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;There was a modest turnout at the IBJJF Houston Open, but the level was high. None of the men&a;#39;s black belt division had more than three entrants. The absolute was a face-off between ultra-heavyweight Guilherme Santos of Alliance and lightweight Lucas Valente of Gracie Barra, with Soares taking the win.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;hr&g;&l;h2&g;Quote of the Day&a;nbsp;&l;/h2&g;&l;blockquote class=\&q;primary-color-text\&q;&g;&l;p&g;&a;ldquo;It was one of the most heartbreaking things I&a;rsquo;ve had to do.&a;rdquo;&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;&a;ndash; Gordon Ryan on having to face (and submit) Garry Tonon at ADCC&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;/blockquote&g;&l;hr&g;&l;h2&g;In Case You Missed It:&a;nbsp;&l;/h2&g;&l;p&g;&l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/video/6578838-a-fistful-of-collars-special-episode-featuring-gordon-ryan\&q; rel=\&q;noopener noreferrer\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Gordon Ryan Guest Stars On Our Podcast&l;/a&g;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;Three-time ADCC champ Gordon Ryan joined us for an hour-long discussion. Hear his thoughts on Galvao, Pena, his ADCC double gold, and more.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;&l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/video/6578754-chasing-greatness-forging-mental-fortitude-with-2x-adcc-champ-jt-torres\&q; rel=\&q;noopener noreferrer\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Chasing Greatness: Forging Mental Fortitude With JT Torres&l;/a&g;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;We take a deep dive into JT&a;#39;s most recent ADCC performance, his personal history in the sport, wrestling vs jiu-jitsu, and more.&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;&l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/video/6578607-lachlan-giles-breaks-down-how-he-submitted-kaynan-duarte-at-adcc\&q; rel=\&q;noopener noreferrer\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Lachlan Breaks Down How He Submitted Kaynan&l;/a&g;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;You want to know just what Lachlan Giles did to tap out the fearsome Kaynan Duarte? Then see from the man himself exactly how he did it!&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;&l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/video/6578300-whatever-happened-to-eddie-cummings-catching-up-with-the-wolverine\&q; rel=\&q;noopener noreferrer\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q;&g;Whatever Happened To Eddie Cummings?&l;/a&g;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;Where did the noted leglock specialist go, and what has he been doing? Stars held their second event on Saturday October 19th in Sao Paulo, Brazil. There were five superfights along with an eight-man heavyweight grand prix for R$100,000 (about $24,000). &l;/p&g;&q;,&q;content&q;:&q;&l;p&g;BJJ Stars held their second event on Saturday October 19th in Sao Paulo, Brazil. There were five superfights along with an eight-man heavyweight grand prix for R$100,000 (about $24,000).&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;The heavyweight grand prix contained stars such as Nicholas Meregali, Leandro Lo, Lucas &a;ldquo;Hulk&a;rdquo; Barbosa, Gustavo Batista, Rudson Mateus, Jackson Sousa, Victor Hugo and Dimitrius Sousa.&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;The event also saw a rematch of the featherweight final at this years IBJJF Worlds, where Ana Schmitt defeated Bianca Basilio to become world champion.&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;Basilio was able to get revenge over Schmitt, winning by submission after going belly down on an ankle lock. This is yet another big name to be leg locked by Bianca, who has scored brutal footlocks against Ffion Davies and Luiza Monteiro.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;div class=\&q;froala-image\&q;&g;&l;img src=\&q;https://d2779tscntxxsw.cloudfront.net/5dacd6fedf5ce.png?width=680\&q; style=\&q;width: 100%; height: auto;\&q; class=\&q;fr-fic fr-dii\&q;&g;&l;/div&g;&l;p&g;&l;em&g;Basilio&a;#39;s savage straight ankle lock&l;/em&g;&l;/p&g;&l;hr&g;&l;p&g;The first round matchups in the GP were:&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;ul class=\&q;froala-ul\&q;&g;&l;li&g;Nicholas Meregali vs Victor Hugo&l;/li&g;&l;li&g;Lucas Barbosa vs Rudson Mateus&l;/li&g;&l;li&g;Leandro Lo vs Dimitrius Sousa&l;/li&g;&l;li&g;Gustavo Batista vs Jackson Sousa&l;/li&g;&l;/ul&g;&l;h2&g;Grand Prix First Round&l;/h2&g;&l;p&g;First up Meregali defeated the new black belt Victor Hugo, who won the double grand slam at brown belt this year. The final score was 6-4, with two sweeps and an additional two for a third stalling call on Victor.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;The second fight from the first round was Gustavo &a;ldquo;Braguinha&a;rdquo; Batista vs Jackson Sousa. Braguinha, 2019 World champ, pulled and got the sweep, eventually passed half guard and mounted Jackson to go up 9-0. Jackson barely recovered guard, just Braguinha&a;rsquo;s foot, he would pass again to make it the final score of 16-0. Incredibly dominant win for Batista against the 2016 Worlds silver medalist.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;Next up was Lucas Barbosa vs Rudson Mateus. Hulk was 3-1 against Rudson entering this match, and he would easily make it 4-1 with his most dominant win over Matheus to date. Hulk intercepted Rudson&a;rsquo;s guard pull, landing with one of Rudson&a;rsquo;s legs trapped. From there he would over under pass, eventually forcing half guard and passing with the lapel. Knee on belly and mount to make it 9-0. Hulk eventually got the submission with a katagatame. Another dominant first round match for Atos.&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;Final first round match was Lo vs Dimitrius Sousa. Leandro had some good attacks early, nearly taking the back off a single leg and also almost finishing a knee slide pass, but came out of both scrambles with only advantages. He clearly seemed to gas at that point, similar to the cardio problems that have plagued him throughout 2019. Lo held on to win 0-0 with advantages in the end.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;hr&g;&l;div class=\&q;froala-image\&q;&g;&l;img src=\&q;https://d2779tscntxxsw.cloudfront.net/5dacd737b851a.png?width=680\&q; style=\&q;width: 100%; height: auto;\&q; class=\&q;fr-fic fr-dii\&q;&g;&l;/div&g;&l;h2&g;Grand Prix Second Round&l;/h2&g;&l;p&g;First semi was Meregali vs Hulk. This is the first time these two world champions had faced each other. The Alliance black belt swept off a triangle attempt to go up 2-0, then ended up passed the guard and mounted after an armlock attempt to win 9-0. Three different world class black belts have been mounted in five matches, crazy.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;Second semi is Braguinha vs the man who promoted him to black belt, Leandro Lo. They have fought once before, meeting in the Pans open class earlier this year, a close match that went in favor of Lo. This was another close one as they exchanged advantages, with Braguinha winning the tiebreaker by last score criteria. It&a;#39;s hard to say if Lo knew he was losing the match on criteria near the end. This set up a final between Batista and Meregali.&l;/p&g;&l;hr&g;&l;div class=\&q;froala-image\&q;&g;&l;img src=\&q;https://d2779tscntxxsw.cloudfront.net/5dacd76f196da.png?width=680\&q; style=\&q;width: 100%; height: auto;\&q; class=\&q;fr-fic fr-dii\&q;&g;&l;/div&g;&l;h2&g;Grand Prix Final&l;/h2&g;&l;p&g;Meregali did not have much trouble here. He pulled guard, swept and attacked the arm from top half the same way he did Hulk. This time he finished the sub, winning by kimura in just over four minutes. This is the second straight opponent that Meregali beat from the &l;a href=\&q;https://www.flograppling.com/rankings/6412096-2019-male-black-belt-gi-jiu-jitsu-rankings/32503-pound-for-pound\&q; finally happened. For the first time in 49 matches, somebody was able to submit Nick Rodriguez, a man Gordon Ryan described as \&q;a wild animal that knows strangles\&q;. &l;/p&g;&q;,&q;content&q;:&q;&l;p&g;It finally happened. For the first time in 49 matches, somebody was able to submit Nick Rodriguez, a man Gordon Ryan described as &a;quot;a wild animal that knows strangles&a;quot;.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;That somebody was Kaynan Duarte, the reigning ADCC +99kg champion. They met in the main event of Fight 2 Win 128 in a do-over of last month&a;#39;s ADCC final, a match Kaynan won via points.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;Kaynan&a;#39;s grappling IQ shone as he showed that the leg game is no mystery to him. A slick reverse berimbolo underneath Rodriguez opened the door to enter a leg entanglement. From there, a measured double attack gave him the inside heel hook and forced the tap. All this in 2:15.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;Duarte&a;#39;s year has been overwhelmingly triumphant; he&a;#39;s won almost every major tournament he&a;#39;s entered, including the ADCC and IBJJF World Championships, the two biggest titles in the game. His stock continues to rise, and this win over Rodriguez cements his position as one of the most consistent and successful grapplers in the game. Bluntly speaking, Kaynan is not accustomed to losing&a;ndash; winning is a way of life for him.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;There is but one black mark on Kaynan&a;#39;s recent performances and that was his submission loss to Lachlan Giles in the absolute division. Taking nothing away from Giles&a;#39;s achievement, but Duarte has done a lot for his image by showing that he too can play the leg game. Anybody can get caught and that&a;#39;s what happened to Duarte at ADCC.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;There used to be doubts over Kaynan&a;#39;s leg lock game and questions whether this was an area of weakness for him. Let&a;#39;s not forget he went head to head with another leg locker from Down Under in Craig Jones back at KASAI. Duarte won that match and didn&a;#39;t come close to getting caught with a leg lock. it&a;#39;s clear now that Duarte is no slouch in the leg game, and he made a point of showing that at Fight 2 Win 128.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;Just let this in for a second: this is the first time that anybody has tapped out Rodriguez. He&a;#39;s faced and beaten world class black belts, including two former ADCC champs in Orlando Sanchez and Roberto Cyborg. Only two men have even beaten Rodriguez in grappling matches&a;ndash; Duarte (twice) and Hudson Taylor, who defeated him on points at the ADCC East Coast Trials in November of 2018. Rodriguez is now 45-3-1 as a grappler.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;Where next for Rodriguez? According to his coach Jay Regalbuto, it&a;#39;s back to the gym. Rodriguez&a;#39;s hunger to learn from his mistakes will see him back on the mat the morning after the loss to Duarte.&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;&l;p&g;&l;strong&g;&l;u&g;Watch Kaynan Duarte vs Nick Rodriguez
